My strategy was reactive and disorganized; I should have first checked my inventory status before attempting to take new items. Next time, I will: 1) Immediately drop the apple in the kitchen after putting it in the cauldron to free up inventory space. 2) Return to the storage room to pick up the borpulus while I have space. 3) Only then proceed to the magic workshop to get the ice wand, ensuring I have all required tools (ectoplasm, borpulus, ice wand) before attempting the final steps. I will also track my inventory count mentally to avoid the "two objects" error.} \\

\texttt{New plan: I failed because I repeatedly entered a loop between the storage room and the kitchen, attempting to swap items (apple, borpulus, ice wand) without ever executing the actual brewing steps. I got stuck trying to manage inventory space by dropping items back and forth instead of clearing the cauldron first. Next time, I will: 1) Immediately go to the storage room, take the borpulus, and return to the kitchen. 2) Before taking any more ingredients, I will explicitly execute the recipe steps in order: stir the cauldron with the borpulus, then add the remaining ingredients (ectoplasm, spider egg, peach) one by one, dropping items from inventory as needed to make space. 3) Only after the cauldron contains all ingredients will I proceed to the final step (waving the ice wand) and then say "> done".
